<img src="https://ad.doubleclick.net/ddm/trackimp/N5295.3091281BUYSELLADS/B34721851.453481127;dc_trk_aid=647254185;dc_trk_cid=246706990;ord=1788445210;dc_lat=;dc_rdid=;tag_for_child_directed_treatment=;tfua=;gdpr=$;gdpr_consent=$;ltd=;dc_tdv=1?" style="display:none;" height="0" width="0"></div></div><h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="prerequisites">Prerequisites<a href="#prerequisites" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Prerequisites" title="Direct link to Prerequisites" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>If you don't use the standalone script or <code>@pnpm/exe</code> to install pnpm, then you need to have Node.js (at least v22) to be installed on your system.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-info admonition_J4jQ alert alert--info"><div class="admonitionHeading_NKiA"><span class="admonitionIcon_pXCe"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M7 2.3c3.14 0 5.7 2.56 5.7 5.7s-2.56 5.7-5.7 5.7A5.71 5.71 0 0 1 1.3 8c0-3.14 2.56-5.7 5.7-5.7zM7 1C3.14 1 0 4.14 0 8s3.14 7 7 7 7-3.14 7-7-3.14-7-7-7zm1 3H6v5h2V4zm0 6H6v2h2v-2z"></path></svg></span>info</div><div class="admonitionContent_mqCC"><p>Looking for pnpm 12? It is released, but <code>latest</code> on npm is still pnpm 11, so it is installed differently. See <a href="#installing-pnpm-12" class="">Installing pnpm 12</a>.</p></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-a-standalone-script">Using a standalone script<a href="#using-a-standalone-script" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using a standalone script" title="Direct link to Using a standalone script" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>You may install pnpm even if you don't have Node.js installed, using the following scripts.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="on-windows">On Windows<a href="#on-windows" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to On Windows" title="Direct link to On Windows" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_J4jQ alert alert--warning"><div class="admonitionHeading_NKiA"><span class="admonitionIcon_pXCe"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"></path></svg></span>warning</div><div class="admonitionContent_mqCC"><p>Sometimes, Windows Defender may block our executable if you install pnpm this way.</p><p>Due to this issue, we currently recommend installing pnpm using <a href="#using-npm" class="">npm</a> on Windows.</p></div></div>
<p>Using PowerShell:</p>
<div class="language-powershell codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-powershell codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">Invoke-WebRequest</span><span class="token plain"> https:</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">/</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">/</span><span class="token plain">get</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">.</span><span class="token plain">pnpm</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">.</span><span class="token plain">io/install</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">.</span><span class="token plain">ps1 </span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">-</span><span class="token plain">UseBasicParsing </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">|</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">Invoke-Expression</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Toggle word wrap" title="Toggle word wrap" class="clean-btn"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="wordWrapButtonIcon_oXB8" aria-hidden="true"><path fill="currentColor" d="M4 19h6v-2H4v2zM20 5H4v2h16V5zm-3 6H4v2h13.25c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2s-.9 2-2 2H15v-2l-3 3l3 3v-2h2c2.21 0 4-1.79 4-4s-1.79-4-4-4z"></path></svg></button><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>On Windows, Microsoft Defender can significantly slow down installation of packages. You can add pnpm to Microsoft Defender's list
of excluded folders in a PowerShell window with administrator rights by executing:</p>
<div class="language-powershell codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-powershell codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">Add-MpPreference</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">-</span><span class="token plain">ExclusionPath $</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">(</span><span class="token plain">pnpm store path</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">)</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="on-posix-systems">On POSIX systems<a href="#on-posix-systems" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to On POSIX systems" title="Direct link to On POSIX systems" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-warning admonition_J4jQ alert alert--warning"><div class="admonitionHeading_NKiA"><span class="admonitionIcon_pXCe"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.893 1.5c-.183-.31-.52-.5-.887-.5s-.703.19-.886.5L.138 13.499a.98.98 0 0 0 0 1.001c.193.31.53.501.886.501h13.964c.367 0 .704-.19.877-.5a1.03 1.03 0 0 0 .01-1.002L8.893 1.5zm.133 11.497H6.987v-2.003h2.039v2.003zm0-3.004H6.987V5.987h2.039v4.006z"></path></svg></span>Not supported on Intel macOS in pnpm 11</div><div class="admonitionContent_mqCC"><p>On pnpm 11, the standalone script does not run on Intel Macs (<code>darwin-x64</code>). Use <a href="#using-homebrew" class="">Homebrew</a>, or install pnpm 12, which ships an Intel build. See <a href="https://github.com/pnpm/pnpm/issues/11423" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">#11423</a> for context.</p><p>pnpm 12 ships an Intel macOS build again, so this limitation doesn't apply to it.</p></div></div>
<div class="language-sh codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-sh codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">curl</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token parameter variable" style="color: #36acaa;">-fsSL</span><span class="token plain"> https://get.pnpm.io/install.sh </span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">|</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">sh</span><span class="token plain"> -</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>If you don't have curl installed, you would like to use wget:</p>
<div class="language-sh codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-sh codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">wget</span><span class="token plain"> -qO- https://get.pnpm.io/install.sh </span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">|</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">sh</span><span class="token plain"> -</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-info admonition_J4jQ alert alert--info"><div class="admonitionHeading_NKiA"><span class="admonitionIcon_pXCe"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M7 2.3c3.14 0 5.7 2.56 5.7 5.7s-2.56 5.7-5.7 5.7A5.71 5.71 0 0 1 1.3 8c0-3.14 2.56-5.7 5.7-5.7zM7 1C3.14 1 0 4.14 0 8s3.14 7 7 7 7-3.14 7-7-3.14-7-7-7zm1 3H6v5h2V4zm0 6H6v2h2v-2z"></path></svg></span>Linux runtime requirements</div><div class="admonitionContent_mqCC"><p>The install script picks a glibc or musl build based on your system's libc, and a separate musl build is provided for Alpine and other musl-based distros. The glibc build requires glibc 2.27 or newer plus <code>libatomic.so.1</code> — both are present on most full distros but may be missing from minimal container images. If you see <code>error while loading shared libraries: libatomic.so.1</code>, install it with your distro's package manager:</p><ul>
<li class="">Debian/Ubuntu: <code>apt-get install -y libatomic1</code></li>
<li class="">Fedora/RHEL: <code>dnf install -y libatomic</code></li>
</ul></div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-tip admonition_J4jQ alert alert--success"><div class="admonitionHeading_NKiA"><span class="admonitionIcon_pXCe"><svg viewBox="0 0 12 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.5 0C3.48 0 1 2.19 1 5c0 .92.55 2.25 1 3 1.34 2.25 1.78 2.78 2 4v1h5v-1c.22-1.22.66-1.75 2-4 .45-.75 1-2.08 1-3 0-2.81-2.48-5-5.5-5zm3.64 7.48c-.25.44-.47.8-.67 1.11-.86 1.41-1.25 2.06-1.45 3.23-.02.05-.02.11-.02.17H5c0-.06 0-.13-.02-.17-.2-1.17-.59-1.83-1.45-3.23-.2-.31-.42-.67-.67-1.11C2.44 6.78 2 5.65 2 5c0-2.2 2.02-4 4.5-4 1.22 0 2.36.42 3.22 1.19C10.55 2.94 11 3.94 11 5c0 .66-.44 1.78-.86 2.48zM4 14h5c-.23 1.14-1.3 2-2.5 2s-2.27-.86-2.5-2z"></path></svg></span>tip</div><div class="admonitionContent_mqCC"><p>You may use the <a class="" href="/cli/runtime">pnpm runtime</a> command then to install Node.js.</p></div></div>

<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="installing-a-specific-version">Installing a specific version<a href="#installing-a-specific-version" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Installing a specific version" title="Direct link to Installing a specific version" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Prior to running the install script, you may optionally set an env variable <code>PNPM_VERSION</code> to install a specific version of pnpm:</p>
<div class="language-sh codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-sh codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">curl</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token parameter variable" style="color: #36acaa;">-fsSL</span><span class="token plain"> https://get.pnpm.io/install.sh </span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">|</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">env</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token assign-left variable" style="color: #36acaa;">PNPM_VERSION</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">=</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">&lt;</span><span class="token plain">version</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">&gt;</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">sh</span><span class="token plain"> -</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-other-package-managers">Using other package managers<a href="#using-other-package-managers" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using other package managers" title="Direct link to Using other package managers" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-npm">Using npm<a href="#using-npm" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using npm" title="Direct link to Using npm" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<div class="language-sh codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-sh codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">npx get-pnpm</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-homebrew">Using Homebrew<a href="#using-homebrew" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using Homebrew" title="Direct link to Using Homebrew" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>If you have the package manager installed, you can install pnpm using the following command:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">brew install pnpm</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-winget">Using winget<a href="#using-winget" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using winget" title="Direct link to Using winget" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>If you have winget installed, you can install pnpm using the following command:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">winget install -e --id pnpm.pnpm</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-scoop">Using Scoop<a href="#using-scoop" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using Scoop" title="Direct link to Using Scoop" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>If you have Scoop installed, you can install pnpm using the following command:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">scoop install nodejs-lts pnpm</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="using-choco">Using Choco<a href="#using-choco" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using Choco" title="Direct link to Using Choco" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>If you have Chocolatey installed, you can install pnpm using the following command:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">choco install pnpm</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-tip admonition_J4jQ alert alert--success"><div class="admonitionHeading_NKiA"><span class="admonitionIcon_pXCe"><svg viewBox="0 0 12 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.5 0C3.48 0 1 2.19 1 5c0 .92.55 2.25 1 3 1.34 2.25 1.78 2.78 2 4v1h5v-1c.22-1.22.66-1.75 2-4 .45-.75 1-2.08 1-3 0-2.81-2.48-5-5.5-5zm3.64 7.48c-.25.44-.47.8-.67 1.11-.86 1.41-1.25 2.06-1.45 3.23-.02.05-.02.11-.02.17H5c0-.06 0-.13-.02-.17-.2-1.17-.59-1.83-1.45-3.23-.2-.31-.42-.67-.67-1.11C2.44 6.78 2 5.65 2 5c0-2.2 2.02-4 4.5-4 1.22 0 2.36.42 3.22 1.19C10.55 2.94 11 3.94 11 5c0 .66-.44 1.78-.86 2.48zM4 14h5c-.23 1.14-1.3 2-2.5 2s-2.27-.86-2.5-2z"></path></svg></span>tip</div><div class="admonitionContent_mqCC"><p>Do you wanna use pnpm on CI servers? <h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="installing-pnpm-12">Installing pnpm 12<a href="#installing-pnpm-12" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Installing pnpm 12" title="Direct link to Installing pnpm 12" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>pnpm 12, the Rust rewrite, is stable since v12.0.0 (August 26, 2026). Please <a href="https://github.com/pnpm/pnpm/issues" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">report any issues</a> you run into.</p>
<p>Apart from a short list of differences, pnpm 12 does not change the way you use pnpm 11, so the rest of this documentation applies to both versions — see <a class="" href="/blog/whats-different-in-pnpm-12">What's different in pnpm 12</a>. Only installation differs: <code>latest</code> on npm still points at the pnpm 11 line, so pnpm 12 is installed from the <code>next-12</code> tag, and Homebrew, winget, Scoop and Chocolatey don't offer it yet.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="pnpm-12-using-pnpm">Using pnpm<a href="#pnpm-12-using-pnpm" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using pnpm" title="Direct link to Using pnpm" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>If you already have pnpm v11.10.0 or newer, this is the easiest way to switch:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">pnpm self-update next-12</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>pnpm links the native binary directly, so nothing else is needed. Note that inside a project that pins pnpm through the <code>packageManager</code> field, <a class="" href="/cli/self-update"><code>self-update</code></a> only updates that pin instead of installing pnpm globally.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="pnpm-12-using-npm">Using npm<a href="#pnpm-12-using-npm" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using npm" title="Direct link to Using npm" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>If you don't have pnpm installed yet:</p>
<div class="language-sh codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-sh codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">npx get-pnpm next-12</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>Node.js 22.13 or newer is needed to run that install script, but not to run pnpm afterwards — pnpm 12 is a native binary.</p>
<h3 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="pnpm-12-using-a-standalone-script">Using a standalone script<a href="#pnpm-12-using-a-standalone-script" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Using a standalone script" title="Direct link to Using a standalone script" translate="no">​</a></h3>
<p>Set <code>PNPM_VERSION</code> to <code>next-12</code>, or to an exact version.</p>
<p>On POSIX systems:</p>
<div class="language-sh codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-sh codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">curl</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token parameter variable" style="color: #36acaa;">-fsSL</span><span class="token plain"> https://get.pnpm.io/install.sh </span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">|</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">env</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token assign-left variable" style="color: #36acaa;">PNPM_VERSION</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">=</span><span class="token plain">next-12 </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">sh</span><span class="token plain"> -</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>On Windows, using PowerShell:</p>
<div class="language-powershell codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-powershell codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token variable" style="color: #36acaa;">$env</span><span class="token plain">:PNPM_VERSION=</span><span class="token string" style="color: #e3116c;">"next-12"</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">;</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">Invoke-WebRequest</span><span class="token plain"> https:</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">/</span><span class="token operator" style="color: #393A34;">/</span><span class="token plain">get</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">.</span><span class="token plain">pnpm</span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">.</span><span class="token plain">io/install</span><span </span><span class="token punctuation" style="color: #393A34;">|</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color: #d73a49;">Invoke-Expression</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Toggle word wrap" title="Toggle word wrap" class="clean-btn"><svg viewBox="0 0 4-4s-1.79-4-4-4z"></path></svg></button><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 <p>This installs pnpm without requiring Node.js, and unlike pnpm 11 it also works on Intel macOS.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="compatibility">Compatibility<a href="#compatibility" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Compatibility" title="Direct link to Compatibility" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Here is a list of past pnpm versions with respective Node.js version support.</p>
<table><thead><tr><th>Node.js</th><th>pnpm 8</th><th>pnpm 9</th><th>pnpm 10</th><th>pnpm 11</th><th>pnpm 12</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Node.js 14</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td></tr><tr><td>Node.js 16</td><td>✔️</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td><td>❌</td></tr><tr><td>Node.js 18</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>❌</td><td>✔️</td></tr><tr><td>Node.js 20</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>❌</td><td>✔️</td></tr><tr><td>Node.js 22</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td></tr><tr><td>Node.js 24</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td></tr><tr><td>Node.js 26</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td><td>✔️</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>pnpm 12 only needs Node.js when it is installed from npm; the version installed by the standalone script runs without Node.js.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="troubleshooting">Troubleshooting<a href="#troubleshooting" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Troubleshooting" title="Direct link to Troubleshooting" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>If pnpm is broken and you cannot fix it by reinstalling, you might need to remove it manually from the PATH.</p>
<p>Let's assume you have the following error when running <code>pnpm install</code>:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">C:\src&gt;pnpm install</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">internal/modules/cjs/loader.js:883</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">  throw err;</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: Function.Module._load (internal/modules/cjs/loader.js:725:27)←[39m</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">←[90m    at Function.executeUserEntryPoint [as runMain] (internal/modules/run_main.js:72:12)←[39m</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">←[90m    at internal/main/run_main_module.js:17:47←[39m {</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">  code: ←[32m'MODULE_NOT_FOUND'←[39m,</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span type="button" aria-label="Toggle word wrap" title="Toggle word wrap" class="clean-btn"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="wordWrapButtonIcon_oXB8" aria-hidden="true"><path fill="currentColor" d="M4 19h6v-2H4v2zM20 5H4v2h16V5zm-3 6H4v2h13.25c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2s-.9 2-2 2H15v-2l-3 3l3 3v-2h2c2.21 0 4-1.79 4-4s-1.79-4-4-4z"></path></svg></button><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>First, try to find the location of pnpm by running: <code>which pnpm</code>. If you're on Windows, run <code>where.exe pnpm.*</code>.
You'll get the location of the pnpm command, for instance:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">$ which pnpm</span><br></div><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">/c/Program Files/nodejs/pnpm</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<p>Now that you know where the pnpm CLI is, open that directory and remove any pnpm-related files (<code>pnpm.cmd</code>, <code>pnpx.cmd</code>, <code>pnpm</code>, etc).
Once done, install pnpm again and it should work as expected.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="updating-pnpm">Updating pnpm<a href="#updating-pnpm" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Updating pnpm" title="Direct link to Updating pnpm" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>To update pnpm, run the <a class="" href="/cli/self-update"><code>self-update</code></a> command:</p>
<div class="language-text codeBlockContainer_rlFr theme-code-block" style="--prism-color: #393A34; --prism-background-color: #f6f8fa;"><div class="codeBlockContent_qRwH"><pre tabindex="0" class="prism-code language-text codeBlock_DOCH thin-scrollbar" style="color: #393A34; background-color: #f6f8fa;"><code class="codeBlockLines_d4DD"><div class="token-line" style="color: #393A34;"><span class="token plain">pnpm self-update</span><br></div></code></pre><div class="buttonGroup_tJzk"><button type="button" aria-label="Copy code to clipboard" title="Copy" class="clean-btn"><span class="copyButtonIcons_t2AR" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonIcon_sx5c"><path fill="currentColor" d="M19,21H8V7H19M19,5H8A2,2 0 0,0 6,7V21A2,2 0 0,0 8,23H19A2,2 0 0,0 21,21V7A2,2 0 0,0 19,5M16,1H4A2,2 0 0,0 2,3V17H4V3H16V1Z"></path></svg><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="copyButtonSuccessIcon_3iSC"><path fill="currentColor" d="M21,7L9,19L3.5,13.5L4.91,12.09L9,16.17L19.59,5.59L21,7Z"></path></svg></span></button></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_uzTO" id="uninstalling-pnpm">Uninstalling pnpm<a href="#uninstalling-pnpm" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Uninstalling pnpm" title="Direct link to Uninstalling pnpm" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>If you need to remove the pnpm CLI from your system and any files it has written to your disk, see <a class="" href="/uninstall">Uninstalling pnpm</a>.</p><script src="//m.servedby-buysellads.com/monetization.custom.js"></script><script>
